## G. Speaking Prompts

1. Describe a difficult learning experience and explain how you persevered.
2. Describe a scenic or far-flung place you have visited or want to visit.
3. Explain a social or environmental problem using evidence, causes, and a practical remedy.
4. Talk about a family role or way of life that has changed these days.

## H. Writing Prompts

1. Write 120–150 words about how technology can improve education and the standard of living. Use at least eight target entries.
2. Write 120–150 words about eco-tourism and endangered wildlife. Include at least five collocations from the workbook.
3. Write a short narrative about adversity, family support, and recovery. Use at least four different word-family forms.

## Final Answer Key

### A. Mixed Test

1. **bully** — A bully often targets people who seem vulnerable. *(countable noun; transitive verb)*
2. **superior** — Public transport is often superior to private cars in crowded city centres. *(adjective; countable noun)*
3. **chipped** — The cup is chipped, so be careful when you drink from it. *(adjective; verb form)*
4. **once-in-a-lifetime** — For many travelers, seeing the northern lights is a once-in-a-lifetime experience. *(adjective phrase; adverb phrase)*
5. **elementary** — Children should develop strong reading habits in elementary school. *(adjective)*
6. **recount** — Witnesses were asked to recount what they saw. *(transitive verb; countable noun)*
7. **eco-tourism** — Eco-tourism can create jobs while protecting natural scenery. *(uncountable noun)*
8. **sleepy** — I felt sleepy after the long ferry ride. *(adjective)*
9. **awe** — Visitors stood in awe of the ancient temple. *(uncountable noun; transitive verb)*
10. **dentistry** — She wants to study dentistry at university. *(uncountable noun)*
11. **gaze** — His gaze moved slowly across the landscape. *(intransitive verb; countable noun)*
12. **missile** — The country invested heavily in missile defence systems. *(countable noun)*
13. **puncture** — We had a puncture on the way to the countryside. *(countable noun; transitive verb)*
14. **telegraph** — The telegraph changed long-distance communication in the nineteenth century. *(countable noun; transitive verb)*
15. **conduct** — Teachers are expected to maintain professional conduct at school. *(transitive verb; uncountable noun)*
16. **estuary** — Many birds feed in the estuary at low tide. *(countable noun)*
17. **roar** — We could hear the roar of the waves from our hotel room. *(countable noun; intransitive verb; transitive verb)*
18. **breadwinner** — After his father became ill, he became the family's main breadwinner. *(countable noun)*
19. **bedridden** — After the stroke, he was bedridden for several months. *(adjective)*
20. **echo** — Online echo chambers can make people less open to opposing views. *(countable noun; transitive verb; intransitive verb)*

### B. Collocation Recall

1–K. **fade away**
2–L. **dented car**
3–M. **natural risk-taker**
4–N. **correspond to something**
5–O. **promote eco-tourism**
6–A. **a stretch of road/coast**
7–B. **sharp decline**
8–C. **gaze at the sky**
9–D. **consult a physician**
10–E. **spare time**
11–F. **orchestra conductor**
12–G. **frighten children**
13–H. **spinal cord injury**
14–I. **beg for mercy**
15–J. **stuck in a rut**

### C. Word-Family Transformation

1. **hall** (family form: **hall**) — The school hall is used for meetings and performances.  
   → Hội trường của trường được dùng cho các cuộc họp và buổi biểu diễn. *(noun)*
2. **Recreational** (family form: **recreational**) — Recreational activities can reduce stress among students.  
   → Các hoạt động giải trí có thể giảm căng thẳng cho học sinh. *(adjective)*
3. **Stuttering** (family form: **stuttering**) — Stuttering should be treated with patience, not ridicule.  
   → Chứng nói lắp nên được đối xử bằng sự kiên nhẫn, không phải sự chế giễu. *(noun/adjective/verb form)*
4. **surfers** (family form: **surfer**) — Experienced surfers know how to read the waves.  
   → Những người lướt sóng có kinh nghiệm biết cách quan sát sóng. *(noun)*
5. **flaunting** (family form: **flaunting**) — The flaunting of wealth can create resentment.  
   → Việc khoe của có thể tạo ra sự oán giận. *(noun/verb form)*
6. **Polar** (family form: **polar**) — Polar regions are highly sensitive to climate change.  
   → Các vùng cực rất nhạy cảm với biến đổi khí hậu. *(adjective)*
7. **Fire** (family form: **fire**) — Fire can spread quickly in dry forests.  
   → Lửa có thể lan nhanh trong rừng khô. *(noun/verb)*
8. **Grappling** (family form: **grappling**) — Grappling with difficult ideas can improve critical thinking.  
   → Việc vật lộn với các ý tưởng khó có thể cải thiện tư duy phản biện. *(noun/verb form)*
9. **distressed** (family form: **distressed**) — The child appeared distressed and asked for her mother.  
   → Đứa trẻ có vẻ đau khổ và hỏi tìm mẹ. *(adjective)*
10. **superiority** (family form: **superiority**) — A sense of superiority can damage teamwork.  
   → Cảm giác hơn người có thể làm tổn hại tinh thần làm việc nhóm. *(noun)*

### D. Error Correction

1. Her work is superior to mine.  
   *Dùng `superior to`, không dùng `superior than`.*
2. The afternoon flew by very quickly.  
   *Cụm đúng là `fly by`.*
3. This trip is a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity.  
   *Dùng dấu gạch nối khi cụm đứng trước danh từ như một tính từ.*
4. The findings correspond to the earlier estimate.  
   *`Correspond to` diễn tả sự tương ứng; `correspond with` thường nói về trao đổi thư từ hoặc cũng có thể chỉ sự phù hợp trong một số ngữ cảnh.*
5. We should book the ferry in advance.  
   *Cụm cố định là `in advance`.*
6. The scenery was absolutely superb.  
   *`Scenery` là danh từ không đếm được và đi với động từ số ít.*
7. The old system gave way to a digital service.  
   *Cấu trúc đúng là `A gives way to B`.*
8. These days, she no longer works there.  
   *`No longer` đã mang nghĩa phủ định, nên không thêm `doesn't`.*

### E. Paragraph Completion

**Answer:** These days, a far-flung clinic is no longer assumed to be inferior simply because it is remote. Researchers collect empirical evidence to ascertain which services are likely to improve patients' standard of living.

### F. Vietnamese-to-English Translation

1. Water dribbled from the old pipe onto the floor.  
   → Nước nhỏ giọt từ ống nước cũ xuống sàn.
2. The IELTS test seems daunting at first, but practice makes it manageable.  
   → Kỳ thi IELTS ban đầu có vẻ đáng sợ, nhưng luyện tập khiến nó dễ xử lý hơn.
3. The island is famous for its crystal-clear water.  
   → Hòn đảo nổi tiếng với làn nước trong vắt.
4. Traditional notebooks are an analogue alternative to digital apps.  
   → Sổ tay truyền thống là một lựa chọn tương tự thay cho ứng dụng kỹ thuật số.
5. She sat by the canal and gazed into the water.  
   → Cô ấy ngồi bên kênh và nhìn chăm chú xuống nước.
6. The company promised to remedy the safety problems quickly.  
   → Công ty hứa sẽ khắc phục các vấn đề an toàn nhanh chóng.
7. The climber placed his hand in a narrow crevice.  
   → Người leo núi đặt tay vào một khe hẹp.
8. A sudden hike in tuition fees can put pressure on students.  
   → Việc tăng học phí đột ngột có thể gây áp lực cho sinh viên.
9. It is likely that remote work will remain common in the future.  
   → Có khả năng làm việc từ xa sẽ vẫn phổ biến trong tương lai.
10. A cooler box is useful for picnics because it keeps drinks cold.  
   → Thùng giữ lạnh rất hữu ích cho các buổi dã ngoại vì nó giữ đồ uống lạnh.

### G. Speaking Prompts

**Model opening:** One challenging experience taught me to persevere when progress seemed slow. I first ascertained the cause of the problem, chose a straightforward remedy, and asked people close to me for support.

Other natural spoken answers are possible. Aim for accurate collocations and clear links between ideas.

### H. Writing Prompts

**Model thesis:** These days, digital technology can improve access to education and raise living standards, provided that governments use empirical evidence to address inequality.

Other well-organized answers are possible. Check word form, countability, prepositions, and register during revision.
